1. idāni kusalādīnaṃ padānaṃ paṭikkhaipavasaina dhammānaṃ pac‍canīyatāya laddhanāmaṃ pac‍canīyapaṭṭhānaṃ dassaituṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayātiādi āraddhaṃ| tattha nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍cāti kusalassa pac‍cayabhāvaṃ vāraiti| nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jatīti kusalassa uppattiṃ vāraiti, tasmā ‘‘akusalābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalābyākatā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpa’’nti aivamādinā nayainaittha pañhaṃ vissaj‍jitabbaṃ| tasmiṃ tasmiṃ pac‍cayai laddhagaṇanā pana pāḷiyaṃ vuttāyaiva| yaipi vārā sadisavissaj‍janā, taipi tatthaiva dassitā| tasmā sabbamaittha haiṭṭhā vuttanayānusāraina  pāḷiṃ upaparikkhitvā vaiditabbaṃ| yathā caittha, aivaṃ dukapaṭṭhānai, dukatikapaṭṭhānai, tikadukapaṭṭhānai tikatikapaṭṭhānai, dukadukapaṭṭhānai ca|

aittāvatā  –

tikañ‍ca paṭṭhānavaraṃ dukuttamaṃ,

dukaṃ tikañ‍caiva tikaṃ dukañ‍ca|

tikaṃ tikañ‍caiva, dukaṃ dukañ‍ca,

cha pac‍canīyamhi nayā sugambhīrāti|| –

aṭṭhakathāyaṃ  vuttagāthāya dīpitā| dhammapac‍canīyapaṭṭhānai cha nayā niddiṭṭhā haunti| pac‍cayavasaina panaittha aikaikasmiṃ paṭṭhānai anulaumādayau cattārau cattārau nayāti aikaina pariyāyaina catuvīsatinayapaṭimaṇḍitaṃ pac‍canīyapaṭṭhānañ‍ñaiva vaiditabbaṃ|

pac‍canīyapaṭṭhānavaṇṇanā|

13-18. anulaumapac‍canīyapaṭṭhānavaṇṇanā

1. idāni kusalādīsu dhammaisu pac‍cayadhammaṃ appaṭikkhipitvā pac‍cayuppan‍nassa kusalādibhāvapaṭikkhaipavasaina dhammānaṃ anulaumapac‍canīyatāya laddhanāmaṃ anulaumapac‍canīyapaṭṭhānaṃ dassaituṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca na kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayātiādi āraddhaṃ| tattha kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍cāti kusalassa pac‍cayabhāvaṃ anujānāti| na kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jatīti kusalassaiva uppattiṃ vāraiti| tasmā ‘‘kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpa’’ntiādinā nayaina vissaj‍janaṃ dassitaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ pāḷiṃ aulaukaitvā sādhukaṃ sal‍lakkhaitabbaṃ| yampi yaina sadisaṃ, yañ‍ca yattha labbhati, yau ca yaisaṃ vissaj‍janānaṃ yaisu pac‍cayaisu gaṇanaparicchaidau, sau sabbau pāḷiyaṃ dassitau, tasmā pāḷiyaiva aittha atthau| yathā caittha, aivaṃ dukapaṭṭhānādīsupīti|

aittāvatā –

tikañ‍ca  paṭṭhānavaraṃ dukuttamaṃ,

dukaṃ tikañ‍caiva tikaṃ dukañ‍ca|

tikaṃ tikañ‍caiva dukaṃ dukañ‍ca,

cha anulaumapac‍canīyamhi nayā sugambhīrāti|| –

aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttagāthāya dīpitā dhammānulaumapac‍canīyapaṭṭhānai cha nayā niddiṭṭhā haunti| pac‍cayavasaina panaittha aikaikasmiṃ paṭṭhānai anulaumādayau cattārau cattārau nayāti aikaina pariyāyaina catuvīsatinayapaṭimaṇḍitaṃ anulaumapac‍canīyapaṭṭhānañ‍ñaiva vaiditabbaṃ|

anulaumapac‍canīyapaṭṭhānavaṇṇanā|

19-24. pac‍canīyānulaumapaṭṭhānavaṇṇanā

1. idāni kusalādīsu dhammaisu pac‍cayadhammaṃ paṭikkhipitvā pac‍cayuppan‍nassa kusalādibhāvaṃ appaṭikkhaipavasaina dhammānaṃ pac‍canīyānulaumatāya laddhanāmaṃ pac‍canīyānulaumapaṭṭhānaṃ dassaituṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayātiādi āraddhaṃ| tattha nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍cāti kusalassa pac‍cayabhāvaṃ vāraiti| akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jatīti akusalassa uppattiṃ anujānāti| nakusalañhi akusalaṃ abyākataṃ vā, tañ‍ca sahajātapac‍cayaṃ katvā uppaj‍jamānau kusalau nāma natthi, tasmā akusalābyākatavasaina daisanā katā| tattha ‘‘akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā’’ti aivaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vissaj‍janaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayāti ayaṃ pana pañhau ‘‘vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpa’’nti vissaj‍jitauva| iti sabbapañhaisu avissaj‍jitassa atthānurūpaṃ vissaj‍jitassa ca pāḷiāgatamaiva vissaj‍janaṃ| aikaikasmiñ‍ca tikadukai vārappabhaidapac‍cayagaṇanavidhānaṃ sabbaṃ haiṭṭhā vuttanayānusārainaiva vaiditabbaṃ|

aittāvatā ca –

tikañ‍ca paṭṭhānavaraṃ dukuttamaṃ,

dukaṃ tikañ‍caiva tikaṃ dukañ‍ca|

tikaṃ  tikañ‍caiva dukaṃ dukañ‍ca,

cha pac‍canīyānulaumamhi nayā sugambhīrāti|| –

aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttagāthāya dīpitā| dhammapac‍canīyānulaumapaṭṭhānai cha nayā niddiṭṭhā haunti| pac‍cayavasaina panaittha aikaikasmiṃ paṭṭhānai anulaumādayau cattārau cattārau nayāti aikaina pariyāyaina catuvīsatinayapaṭimaṇḍitaṃ pac‍canīyānulaumapaṭṭhānañ‍ñaiva vaiditabbaṃ|

pac‍canīyānulaumapaṭṭhānavaṇṇanā|

aivaṃ  dhammānulaumādivasaina catūsu vāraisu aikaikasmiṃ catuvīsatiyā catuvīsatiyā nayānaṃ vasaina chan‍navuti nayā haunti| tattha pac‍cayanayai aggahaitvā aikaikasmiṃ paṭṭhānai tikadukādīnañ‍ñaiva chan‍naṃ chan‍naṃ nayānaṃ vasainaitaṃ catuvīsatinayapaṭimaṇḍitaṃ samantapaṭṭhānamahāpakaraṇaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| kaici pana ‘‘kusalārammaṇau dhammau akusalārammaṇau dhammau’’tiādinā nayaina ārammaṇamātikaṃ nāma ṭhapaitvā ‘‘kusalārammaṇau dhammau kusalārammaṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau’’tiādinā nayaina ārammaṇapaṭṭhānaṃ nāma dassaitvā aparampi phassādīnaṃ vasainapi phassapaṭṭhānaṃ nāma uddharitvā dassainti| taṃ naiva pāḷiyaṃ na aṭṭhakathāsu sandissatīti idha na vicāritaṃ| saṅgītiāruḷhapāḷivasainaiva panaittha vaṇṇanā katāti vaiditabbā|

aittāvatā ca –

sammūḷhā yattha pajā, tantākulādibhāvamāpan‍nā|

naikavidhadukkhagahanaṃ, saṃsāraṃ nātivattanti||

pac‍cayabhaidai kusalau, laukai garutampi pac‍cayākāraṃ|

atinippuṇagambhīraṃ, javanabbhūmiṃ buddhañāṇassa||

kusalādidhammabhaidaṃ, nissāya nayaihi vividhagaṇanaihi|

vitthāraintau sattama-mabhidhammappakaraṇaṃ satthā||

suvihitasan‍niṭṭhānau, paṭṭhānaṃ nāma yaṃ pakāsaisi|

saddhāya samāraddhā, yā aṭṭhakathā mayā tassa||

ācariyānaṃ vādaṃ, avihāya vibhaj‍javādisissānaṃ|

atibahuvidhantarāyai, laukamhi anantarāyaina||

sā aivaṃ aj‍ja katā, cuddasamattaihi bhāṇavāraihi|

atthaṃ pakāsayantī, paṭṭhānavarassa sakalassa||

san‍niṭṭhānaṃ pattā yathaiva niṭṭhaṃ tathā bahujanassa|

sampāpuṇantu sīghaṃ, kalyāṇā sabbasaṅkappā||

aittāvatā  –

sattappakaraṇaṃ nāthau, abhidhammamadaisayi|

daivātidaivau daivānaṃ, daivalaukamhi yaṃ purai||

tassa aṭṭhakathā aisā, sakalassāpi niṭṭhitā|

ciraṭṭhitatthaṃ dhammassa, niṭṭhapaintaina taṃ mayā||

yaṃ pattaṃ kusalaṃ tassa, ānubhāvaina pāṇinau|

sabbai saddhammarājassa, ñatvā dhammaṃ sukhāvahaṃ||

pāpuṇantu visuddhāya, sukhāya paṭipattiyā|

asaukamanupāyāsaṃ, nibbānasukhamuttamaṃ||

ciraṃ tiṭṭhatu saddhammau, dhammai hauntu sagāravā|

sabbaipi sattā kālaina, sammā daivau pavassatu||

yathā  rakkhiṃsu paurāṇā, surājānau tathaivimaṃ|

rājā rakkhatu dhammaina, attanauva pajaṃ pajanti||

paṭṭhānappakaraṇa-aṭṭhakathā niṭṭhitā|

niṭṭhitā ca pañ‍capakaraṇaṭṭhakathāti|

nigamanakathā

paramavisuddhasaddhābuddhivīriyapaṭimaṇḍitaina  sīlācāraj‍javamaddavādiguṇasamudayasamuditaina sakasamayasamayantaragahanajjhaugāhaṇasamatthaina pañ‍ñāvaiyyattiyasaman‍nāgataina tipiṭakapariyattippabhaidai sāṭṭhakathai satthusāsanai appaṭihatañāṇappabhāvaina mahāvaiyyākaraṇaina karaṇasampattijanitasukhaviniggatamadhuraudāravacanalāvaṇṇayuttaina yuttamuttavādinā vādīvaraina mahākavinā pabhin‍napaṭisambhidāparivārai chaḷabhiñ‍ñādippabhaidaguṇapaṭimaṇḍitai uttarimanussadhammai suppatiṭṭhitabuddhīnaṃ thairavaṃsappadīpānaṃ thairānaṃ mahāvihāravāsīnaṃ vaṃsālaṅkārabhūtaina vipulavisuddhabuddhinā buddhaghausauti garūhi gahitanāmadhaiyyaina thairaina katā ayaṃ sakalassapi abhidhammapiṭakassa aṭṭhakathā –

tāva tiṭṭhatu laukasmiṃ, laukanittharaṇaisinaṃ|

dassaintī kulaputtānaṃ, nayaṃ pañ‍ñāvisuddhiyā||

yāva buddhauti nāmampi, suddhacittassa tādinau|

laukamhi laukajaiṭṭhassa, pavattati mahaisinauti||

abhidhammapiṭaka-aṭṭhakathā niṭṭhitā|
